Cross-CLI by design. ([biefan/anchor](https://github.com/biefan/anchor))

- **andie**: Andie - the sharpest thinking partner inside Claude Code. Andie doesn't summarize — it debates, war-games, and dissects until the answer survives scrutiny. Four modes: Deep (world-class expert breaks it down Feynman-style), Drama (expert panel fights your decision to a conclusion), Triage (war strategy applied to your crisis in real time), Kaizen (finds the waste, fixes one thing, locks it in). Automatically picks up and loads specialist skills — AWS-GCP-Azure-OCI, multiple tools like Kafka, Postgres, security, ML, and more — so every answer comes from someone who's actually done the work. ([giggsoinc/andie](https://github.com/giggsoinc/andie))
- **andon**: Evidence-grounded harden-and-advance loop for live, actively-maintained codebases: propose maximally from the code/artifacts/best-practice, verify adversarially against evidence rather than authority across seven proof strategies chosen by wire type, and advance only past a proven wire (the andon rule) — never past a broken or unproven one. Composable skill family (preflight, propose, verify, loop, status) over a native OKF ledger, read-only except a scoped output directory. ([Anselmoo/werkstoff](https://github.com/Anselmoo/werkstoff/tree/HEAD/plugins/andon))
